or target can't be found\n            if (!contentNode || !document.querySelector(targetSelector)) {\n                return;\n            }\n\n                        hyva.activateScripts(contentNode);\n            \n            // Replace the old DOM node with the new content\n            document.querySelector(targetSelector).replaceWith(contentNode);\n\n }\n\n        hyva.removeScripts = (contentNode) => {\n            const scripts = contentNode.getElementsByTagName('script');\n            for (let i = 0; i < scripts.length; i++) {\n                scripts[i].parentNode.removeChild(scripts[i]);\n            }\n            const templates = contentNode.getElementsByTagName('template');\n            for (let i = 0; i < templates.length; i++) {\n                const container = document.createElement('div');\n                container.innerHTML = templates[i].innerHTML;\n                hyva.removeScripts(container);\n                templates[i].innerHTML = container.innerHTML;\n            }\n        }\n\n        hyva.activateScripts = (contentNode) => {\n            // Create new array from HTMLCollection to avoid mutation of collection while manipulating the DOM.\n            const scripts = Array.from(contentNode.getElementsByTagName('script'));\n\n            // Iterate over all script tags to duplicate+inject each into the head\n            for (const original of scripts) {\n                const script = document.createElement('script');\n                script.innerHTML = original.innerHTML;\n\n                // Remove the original (non-executing) script from the contentNode\n                original.parentNode.removeChild(original)\n\n                // Add script to head\n                document.head.appendChild(script);\n            }\n\n            return contentNode;\n        }\n\n        const replace = {['+']: '-', ['/']: '_', ['=']: ','};\n        hyva.getUenc = () => btoa(window.location.href).replace(/[+/=]/g, match => replace[match]);\n\n        let currentTrap;\n\n        const focusableElements = (rootElement) => {\n            const selector = 'button, el.getClientRects().length)\n                })\n        }\n\n        const focusTrap = (e) => {\n            const isTabPressed = e.key === 'Tab' || e.keyCode === 9;\n            if (!isTabPressed) return;\n\n            const focusable = focusableElements(currentTrap)\n            const firstFocusableElement = focusable[0]\n            const lastFocusableElement = focusable[focusable.length - lastFocusableElement && (firstFocusableElement.focus(), e.preventDefault())\n        };\n\n        hyva.releaseFocus = (rootElement) => {\n            if (currentTrap && (!rootElement || rootElement === currentTrap)) {\n                currentTrap.removeEventListener('keydown', focusTrap)\n                currentTrap = null\n            }\n        }\n        hyva.trapFocus = (rootElement) => {\n parseFloat(rawValue) : null;\n\n            return Array.isArray(number) || isNaN(number) ? rawValue : number;\n        }\n\n        const toCamelCase = s => s.split('_').map(word => word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1)).join('');\n        hyva.createBooleanObject = (name, value = false, additionalMethods = {}) => {\n\n            const camelCase = toCamelCase(name);\n            const key = '__hyva_bool_' + name\n\n            return new Proxy(Object.assign(\n                additionalMethods,\n                {\n                    [key]: !!value,\n                    [name]() {return !!this[key]},\n                    ['!' + name]() {return !this[key]},\n                    ['toggle' + camelCase]() {this[key] = !this[key]},\n                    [`set${camelCase}True`]() {this[key] = true},\n                    [`set${camelCase}False`]() {this[key] = false},\n                }\n            ), {\n                set(target, prop, value) {\n                    return prop === name\n                        ?
